## Concrete-only rules

- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:25` (concrete): rule intFloatDiv(I:Int, F:Float) => Int2Float(I, 53, 11) /Float F [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:31` (concrete): rule divII(I1:Int, I2:Int) => Int2Float(I1, 53, 11) /Float Int2Float(I2, 53, 11) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:38` (concrete): rule floatMod(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 -Float (floorFloat(F1 /Float F2) *Float F2) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:51` (concrete): rule floatLt(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 <Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:55` (concrete): rule absF(F:Float) => absFloat(F) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:74` (concrete): rule floorFI(I:Int)   => I                        [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:75` (concrete): rule floorFI(F:Float) => Float2Int(floorFloat(F)) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:87` (concrete): rule toF(F:Float) => F        [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:88` (concrete): rule toF(I:Int)   => intToF(I) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:94` (concrete): rule ceilF(I:Int)   => I                       [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:95` (concrete): rule ceilF(F:Float) => Float2Int(ceilFloat(F)) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:104` (concrete): rule subF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 -Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:108` (concrete): rule divF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 /Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:112` (concrete): rule addF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 +Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:116` (concrete): rule mulF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 *Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:120` (concrete): rule powF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 ^Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:126` (concrete): rule gtF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 >Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:143` (concrete): rule eqF(F1:Float, F2:Float) => F1 ==Float F2 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:161` (concrete): rule decStrToF(iCons(45, CS:IntSeq)) => 0.0 -Float decStrToF(CS) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:162` (concrete): rule decStrToF(CS:IntSeq) => intToF(intPart(CS)) +Float (intToF(fracPart(CS)) /Float intToF(fracScale(CS))) requires isLen(CS) >Int 0 andBool headIS(CS) =/=Int 45 [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:191` (concrete): rule divFloatIntV(F:Float, I:Int) => F /Float Int2Float(I, 53, 11) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:196` (concrete): rule intToF(I:Int) => Int2Float(I, 53, 11) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:210` (concrete): rule truncF(F:Float) => #if F >=Float 0.0 #then Float2Int(floorFloat(F)) #else Float2Int(ceilFloat(F)) #fi [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:218` (concrete): rule roundF(F:Float) => #if (F -Float floorFloat(F)) ==Float 0.5 #then (#if Float2Int(floorFloat(F)) %Int 2 ==Int 0 #then Float2Int(floorFloat(F)) #else Float2Int(ceilFloat(F)) #fi) #else Float2Int(floorFloat(F +Float 0.5)) #fi [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:224` (concrete): rule roundFN(F:Float, N:Int) => Int2Float(roundF(F *Float Int2Float(10 ^Int N, 53, 11)), 53, 11) /Float Int2Float(10 ^Int N, 53, 11) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/float.k:231` (concrete): rule sqrtF(F:Float) => sqrtFloat(F) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:20` (concrete): rule sortVS(.ValSeq)                => .ValSeq          [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:21` (concrete): rule sortVS(vCons(X:Int, R:ValSeq)) => insVS(X, sortVS(R)) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:22` (concrete): rule insVS(X:Int, .ValSeq)                => vCons(X, .ValSeq) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:23` (concrete): rule insVS(X:Int, vCons(Y:Int, R:ValSeq)) => vCons(X, vCons(Y, R)) requires X <=Int Y [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:24` (concrete): rule insVS(X:Int, vCons(Y:Int, R:ValSeq)) => vCons(Y, insVS(X, R)) requires X  >Int Y [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:27` (concrete): rule sortVS(vCons(str(CS:IntSeq), R:ValSeq)) => insVSs(CS, sortVS(R)) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:28` (concrete): rule insVSs(A:IntSeq, .ValSeq) => vCons(str(A), .ValSeq) [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:29` (concrete): rule insVSs(A:IntSeq, vCons(str(B:IntSeq), R:ValSeq)) => vCons(str(A), vCons(str(B), R)) requires strLt(A, B) orBool A ==K B [concrete]
- `reference-semantics/semantics/sort.k:31` (concrete): rule insVSs(A:IntSeq, vCons(str(B:IntSeq), R:ValSeq)) => vCons(str(B), insVSs(A, R)) requires notBool (strLt(A, B) orBool A ==K B) [concrete]
